1	import { PlayCircleOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
2	import { Alert, Button, Empty, Flex, Select, Spin, Table, Tabs, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';
3	import type { ColumnsType } from 'antd/es/table';
4	import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
5	import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
6	
7	import {
8	  listPipelineRuns,
9	  listRuns,
10	  type PipelineRun,
11	  type Run,
12	  type RunStatus,
13	  RUN_STATUSES,
14	} from '../api/runs';
15	import { listSuites } from '../api/suites';
16	import { RunNowPanel } from '../components/runs/RunNowPanel';
17	import { useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';
18	import {
19	  formatDuration,
20	  formatTimestamp,
21	  pipelineStatusColor,
22	  RUN_STATUS_COLORS,
23	} from '../components/results/resultsFormat';
24	
25	const LIST_LIMIT = 200;
26	
27	export function Results() {
28	  const [runNowOpen, setRunNowOpen] = useState(false);
29	  return (
30	    <Flex vertical gap={24}>
31	      <Flex justify="space-between" align="center" gap={12}>
32	        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
33	          Results
34	        </Typography.Title>
35	        <Button type="primary" icon={<PlayCircleOutlined />} onClick={() => setRunNowOpen(true)}>
36	          Run now
37	        </Button>
38	      </Flex>
39	      <RunNowPanel open={runNowOpen} onClose={() => setRunNowOpen(false)} />
40	      <Tabs
41	        defaultActiveKey="runs"
42	        items={[
43	          { key: 'runs', label: 'Runs', children: <RunsTab /> },
44	          { key: 'pipelines', label: 'Pipeline runs', children: <PipelineRunsTab /> },
45	        ]}
46	      />
47	    </Flex>
48	  );
49	}
50	
51	// ───────────────────────────── Runs tab ─────────────────────────────
52	
53	function RunsTab() {
54	  // Fetch a page of runs + the accessible suites (for id→name), then filter by
55	  // status client-side — cheap at this volume and avoids a refetch per filter.
56	  const navigate = useNavigate();
57	  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => listRuns({ limit: LIST_LIMIT }));
58	  const { state: suitesState } = useAsyncData(listSuites);
59	  const [status, setStatus] = useState<RunStatus | 'all'>('all');
60	
61	  const suiteNames = useMemo(() => {
62	    const map = new Map<string, string>();
63	    if (suitesState.status === 'ok') {
64	      for (const s of suitesState.data) map.set(s.id, s.name);
65	    }
66	    return map;
67	  }, [suitesState]);
68	
69	  if (state.status === 'loading') return <Spin tip="Loading runs…" size="large" />;
70	  if (state.status === 'error') {
71	    return <Alert type="error" showIcon message="Failed to load runs" description={state.error} />;
72	  }
73	
74	  const runs = status === 'all' ? state.data : state.data.filter((r) => r.status === status);
75	
76	  const columns: ColumnsType<Run> = [
77	    {
78	      title: 'Suite',
79	      dataIndex: 'suite_id',
80	      render: (suiteId: string) =>
81	        suiteNames.get(suiteId) ?? <Typography.Text code>{suiteId.slice(0, 8)}</Typography.Text>,
82	    },
83	    {
84	      title: 'Status',
85	      dataIndex: 'status',
86	      width: 120,
87	      render: (s: RunStatus) => <Tag color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[s]}>{s}</Tag>,
88	    },
89	    { title: 'Triggered by', dataIndex: 'triggered_by', render: (t: string | null) => t ?? '—' },
90	    {
91	      title: 'Started',
92	      dataIndex: 'started_at',
93	      render: (t: string | null) => formatTimestamp(t),
94	    },
95	    {
96	      title: 'Duration',
97	      width: 110,
98	      render: (_: unknown, run: Run) => formatDuration(run.started_at, run.finished_at),
99	    },
100	  ];
101	
102	  return (
103	    <Flex vertical gap={16}>
104	      <Flex gap={12} align="center">
105	        <Typography.Text type="secondary">Status</Typography.Text>
106	        <Select<RunStatus | 'all'>
107	          value={status}
108	          onChange={setStatus}
109	          style={{ width: 160 }}
110	          options={[
111	            { value: 'all', label: 'All' },
112	            ...RUN_STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: s })),
113	          ]}
114	        />
115	      </Flex>
116	      <Table<Run>
117	        rowKey="id"
118	        columns={columns}
119	        dataSource={runs}
120	        pagination={false}
121	        locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description="No runs yet." /> }}
122	        onRow={(run) => ({
123	          onClick: () => navigate(`/results/${run.id}`),
124	          style: { cursor: 'pointer' },
125	        })}
126	      />
127	    </Flex>
128	  );
129	}
130	
131	// ─────────────────────────── Pipeline runs tab ──────────────────────
132	
133	function PipelineRunsTab() {
134	  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => listPipelineRuns({ limit: LIST_LIMIT }));
135	  const [provider, setProvider] = useState<'all' | 'adf' | 'airflow'>('all');
136	
137	  if (state.status === 'loading') return <Spin tip="Loading pipeline runs…" size="large" />;
138	  if (state.status === 'error') {
139	    return (
140	      <Alert
141	        type="error"
142	        showIcon
143	        message="Failed to load pipeline runs"
144	        description={state.error}
145	      />
146	    );
147	  }
148	
149	  const rows = provider === 'all' ? state.data : state.data.filter((p) => p.provider === provider);
150	
151	  const columns: ColumnsType<PipelineRun> = [
152	    { title: 'Provider', dataIndex: 'provider', width: 110, render: (p: string) => <Tag>{p}</Tag> },
153	    { title: 'Pipeline / DAG', dataIndex: 'pipeline_or_dag_id' },
154	    { title: 'Env', dataIndex: 'env', width: 80, render: (e: string) => e.toUpperCase() },
155	    {
156	      title: 'Status',
157	      dataIndex: 'status',
158	      width: 110,
159	      render: (s: string) => <Tag color={pipelineStatusColor(s)}>{s}</Tag>,
160	    },
161	    {
162	      title: 'Started',
163	      dataIndex: 'started_at',
164	      render: (t: string | null) => formatTimestamp(t),
165	    },
166	    {
167	      title: 'Failure reason',
168	      dataIndex: 'failure_reason',
169	      render: (r: string | null) => r ?? '—',
170	    },
171	  ];
172	
173	  return (
174	    <Flex vertical gap={16}>
175	      <Flex gap={12} align="center">
176	        <Typography.Text type="secondary">Provider</Typography.Text>
177	        <Select<'all' | 'adf' | 'airflow'>
178	          value={provider}
179	          onChange={setProvider}
180	          style={{ width: 160 }}
181	          options={[
182	            { value: 'all', label: 'All' },
183	            { value: 'adf', label: 'ADF' },
184	            { value: 'airflow', label: 'Airflow' },
185	          ]}
186	        />
187	      </Flex>
188	      <Table<PipelineRun>
189	        rowKey="id"
190	        columns={columns}
191	        dataSource={rows}
192	        pagination={false}
193	        locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description="No pipeline runs monitored yet." /> }}
194	      />
195	    </Flex>
196	  );
197	}

1	import { api } from './client';
2	
3	/**
4	 * Runs / results / pipeline-runs API — the read surface behind the Results page
5	 * (backend `runs.py`, PR-C0b). The DQ-run reads are suite-scoped: the backend
6	 * filters to suites the caller can access, so this client never has to. Manual
7	 * run *triggering* (`runSuite` → `POST /suites/{id}/run`) lives here too, since
8	 * it produces a `Run`.
9	 */
10	
11	/** Run execution lifecycle — `status` is execution, not data quality. */
12	export const RUN_STATUSES = ['queued', 'running', 'succeeded', 'failed', 'cancelled'] as const;
13	export type RunStatus = (typeof RUN_STATUSES)[number];
14	
15	/** Result severity tier (ADR 0005) + the two operational statuses (#122). */
16	export type ResultStatus = 'pass' | 'warn' | 'fail' | 'critical' | 'skip' | 'error';
17	
18	/** Mirrors the backend `RunRead`. */
19	export interface Run {
20	  id: string;
21	  suite_id: string;
22	  status: RunStatus;
23	  triggered_by: string | null;
24	  started_at: string | null;
25	  finished_at: string | null;
26	  created_at: string;
27	}
28	
29	/** Mirrors `ResultRead` — `sample_failures` is withheld by the API (PII, ADR 0018). */
30	export interface Result {
31	  id: string;
32	  check_id: string;
33	  status: ResultStatus;
34	  metric_value: number | null;
35	  duration_ms: number | null;
36	  observed_value: Record<string, unknown> | null;
37	  expected_value: Record<string, unknown> | null;
38	}
39	
40	/** Mirrors `RunDetailRead` — a run plus its result rows. */
41	export interface RunDetail extends Run {
42	  results: Result[];
43	}
44	
45	/** Mirrors `CheckProgressRead` — `status` is null while the check is pending. */
46	export interface CheckProgress {
47	  check_id: string;
48	  name: string;
49	  status: ResultStatus | null;
50	}
51	
52	/**
53	 * Mirrors `RunProgressRead` — the compact live-progress shape the run-progress
54	 * UI polls: run lifecycle + per-check resolution + a status histogram. Lighter
55	 * than the full run+results detail (`getRun`).
56	 */
57	export interface RunProgress {
58	  run_id: string;
59	  suite_id: string;
60	  status: RunStatus;
61	  total_checks: number;
62	  completed_checks: number;
63	  counts: Record<string, number>;
64	  checks: CheckProgress[];
65	  started_at: string | null;
66	  finished_at: string | null;
67	}
68	
69	/** Mirrors `PipelineRunRead` — a monitored orchestrator run (`pipeline_runs` ≠ `runs`). */
70	export interface PipelineRun {
71	  id: string;
72	  provider: 'adf' | 'airflow';
73	  connection_id: string;
74	  provider_run_id: string;
75	  pipeline_or_dag_id: string;
76	  env: string;
77	  status: string;
78	  started_at: string | null;
79	  finished_at: string | null;
80	  failure_reason: string | null;
81	  created_at: string;
82	}
83	
84	export async function listRuns(params?: {
85	  suite_id?: string;
86	  status?: RunStatus;
87	  limit?: number;
88	}): Promise<Run[]> {
89	  const { data } = await api.get<Run[]>('/runs', { params });
90	  return data;
91	}
92	
93	export async function getRun(runId: string): Promise<RunDetail> {
94	  const { data } = await api.get<RunDetail>(`/runs/${runId}`);
95	  return data;
96	}
97	
98	/**
99	 * Trigger a run of a suite (`POST /suites/{id}/run`). Edit-gated; returns the
100	 * queued `Run` (HTTP 202). The backend resolves the suite's target up front, so
101	 * a targetless/misconfigured suite fails with 422, and a broker outage with 503.
102	 */
103	export async function runSuite(suiteId: string): Promise<Run> {
104	  const { data } = await api.post<Run>(`/suites/${suiteId}/run`);
105	  return data;
106	}
107	
108	/**
109	 * Poll a run's live progress (`GET /runs/{id}/progress`). Suite-scoped (view).
110	 * Cheaper than `getRun` — no observed/expected payloads — so it's the call the
111	 * live-progress UI hits on its polling interval.
112	 */
113	export async function getRunProgress(runId: string): Promise<RunProgress> {
114	  const { data } = await api.get<RunProgress>(`/runs/${runId}/progress`);
115	  return data;
116	}
117	
118	/**
119	 * Cancel a non-terminal run (`POST /runs/{id}/cancel`). Edit-gated; returns the
120	 * updated `Run`. An already-finished run → 409. Cancel is cooperative (best-effort
121	 * for an in-flight run), so it may race a fast run to completion.
122	 */
123	export async function cancelRun(runId: string): Promise<Run> {
124	  const { data } = await api.post<Run>(`/runs/${runId}/cancel`);
125	  return data;
126	}
127	
128	export async function listPipelineRuns(params?: {
129	  provider?: 'adf' | 'airflow';
130	  status?: string;
131	  limit?: number;
132	}): Promise<PipelineRun[]> {
133	  const { data } = await api.get<PipelineRun[]>('/pipeline_runs', { params });
134	  return data;
135	}
136

1	import { api } from './client';
2	
3	/**
4	 * Suites API — a suite is a named bundle of DQ checks bound to one connection.
5	 * `connection_id` is set at create and immutable thereafter (re-pointing would
6	 * orphan the child checks), mirroring the backend `suite_service` contract.
7	 */
8	
9	/** Mirrors the backend `SuiteRead` schema. */
10	export interface Suite {
11	  id: string;
12	  name: string;
13	  description: string | null;
14	  connection_id: string;
15	  /** Datasource-shaped run target (#215); null = not yet runnable. */
16	  target: Record<string, unknown> | null;
17	  created_by: string;
18	  /** The caller's effective level — gates per-suite actions (share, delete).
19	   *  `owner`/`admin`/`edit`/`view`; absent on older payloads. */
20	  my_permission?: 'owner' | 'admin' | 'edit' | 'view' | null;
21	}
22	
23	/**
24	 * Owner/admin — may manage shares + delete the suite. The single source for the
25	 * "manage" gate so the suite-detail actions and any other surface never drift.
26	 */
27	export function canManageSuite(suite: Suite): boolean {
28	  return suite.my_permission === 'owner' || suite.my_permission === 'admin';
29	}
30	
31	/**
32	 * The `edit` capability ladder (owner/admin/edit) — may trigger/cancel runs and
33	 * manage triggers/schedules, mirroring the backend `POST /suites/{id}/run` gate.
34	 * Shared by the suite-detail Run button and the cross-suite Run-now panel so the
35	 * runnable-permission policy lives in one place.
36	 */
37	export function canRunSuite(suite: Suite): boolean {
38	  return canManageSuite(suite) || suite.my_permission === 'edit';
39	}
40	
41	/**
42	 * The datasource-shaped identity carried in `Suite.target` (#215): SQL targets
43	 * fill `table`/`schema`/`catalog`, flat-file targets fill `path`/`file_format`.
44	 * The wire shape is an untyped JSONB bag (`Record<string, unknown>`); read it
45	 * through `targetString` so the dry-run preview and column profiler don't each
46	 * re-hand-roll the `typeof x === 'string'` extraction.
47	 */
48	export interface RunTarget {
49	  table?: string;
50	  schema?: string;
51	  catalog?: string;
52	  path?: string;
53	  file_format?: 'csv' | 'parquet';
54	}
55	
56	/** Read one string field out of the untyped run-target bag, or `undefined`. */
57	export function targetString(
58	  target: Record<string, unknown> | null,
59	  key: keyof RunTarget,
60	): string | undefined {
61	  const value = target?.[key];
62	  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined;
63	}
64	
65	/** Mirrors `SuiteCreate` — connection_id is required and immutable. `target` is
66	 *  optional (a suite may be created targetless = not-yet-runnable). */
67	export interface SuiteCreate {
68	  name: string;
69	  description?: string | null;
70	  connection_id: string;
71	  target?: RunTarget | null;
72	}
73	
74	/** Mirrors `SuiteUpdate` — name/description/target are mutable (connection isn't).
75	 *  A `null`/omitted `target` leaves the existing one unchanged (backend semantics:
76	 *  it never clears a target back to NULL). */
77	export interface SuiteUpdate {
78	  name?: string;
79	  description?: string | null;
80	  target?: RunTarget | null;
81	}
82	
83	export async function listSuites(params?: { connection_id?: string }): Promise<Suite[]> {
84	  const { data } = await api.get<Suite[]>('/suites', { params });
85	  return data;
86	}
87	
88	export async function getSuite(id: string): Promise<Suite> {
89	  const { data } = await api.get<Suite>(`/suites/${id}`);
90	  return data;
91	}
92	
93	export async function createSuite(payload: SuiteCreate): Promise<Suite> {
94	  const { data } = await api.post<Suite>('/suites', payload);
95	  return data;
96	}
97	
98	export async function updateSuite(id: string, payload: SuiteUpdate): Promise<Suite> {
99	  const { data } = await api.patch<Suite>(`/suites/${id}`, payload);
100	  return data;
101	}
102	
103	export async function deleteSuite(id: string): Promise<void> {
104	  await api.delete(`/suites/${id}`);
105	}
106	
107	/** Mirrors the backend `CheckRead` schema (read-only here — editor is a later slice). */
108	export interface Check {
109	  id: string;
110	  suite_id: string;
111	  name: string;
112	  kind: string;
113	  expectation_type: string;
114	  config: Record<string, unknown>;
115	  warn_threshold: number | null;
116	  fail_threshold: number | null;
117	  critical_threshold: number | null;
118	}
119	
120	export async function listChecks(suiteId: string): Promise<Check[]> {
121	  const { data } = await api.get<Check[]>(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks`);
122	  return data;
123	}
124	
125	/** Mirrors `CheckCreate` — v1 only authors `kind: 'expectation'` (service-enforced). */
126	export interface CheckCreate {
127	  name: string;
128	  expectation_type: string;
129	  config: Record<string, unknown>;
130	  warn_threshold?: number | null;
131	  fail_threshold?: number | null;
132	  critical_threshold?: number | null;
133	}
134	
135	/** Mirrors `CheckUpdate` — all fields optional; kind is immutable. */
136	export interface CheckUpdate {
137	  name?: string;
138	  expectation_type?: string;
139	  config?: Record<string, unknown>;
140	  warn_threshold?: number | null;
141	  fail_threshold?: number | null;
142	  critical_threshold?: number | null;
143	}
144	
145	export async function createCheck(suiteId: string, payload: CheckCreate): Promise<Check> {
146	  const { data } = await api.post<Check>(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks`, payload);
147	  return data;
148	}

151	  suiteId: string,
152	  checkId: string,
153	  payload: CheckUpdate,
154	): Promise<Check> {
155	  const { data } = await api.patch<Check>(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks/${checkId}`, payload);
156	  return data;
157	}
158	
159	export async function deleteCheck(suiteId: string, checkId: string): Promise<void> {
160	  await api.delete(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks/${checkId}`);
161	}
162	
163	/**
164	 * Mirrors the backend `CheckVersionRead` — one immutable snapshot in a check's
165	 * history (#280). `changed_by_name` is the author's display name (or email),
166	 * resolved server-side; null for a system actor or a removed user.
167	 */
168	export interface CheckVersion {
169	  version_no: number;
170	  name: string;
171	  kind: string;
172	  expectation_type: string;
173	  config: Record<string, unknown>;
174	  warn_threshold: number | null;
175	  fail_threshold: number | null;
176	  critical_threshold: number | null;
177	  changed_by: string | null;
178	  changed_by_name: string | null;
179	  created_at: string;
180	}
181	
182	/** A check's version history, newest first. Requires `view` on the suite. */
183	export async function listCheckVersions(suiteId: string, checkId: string): Promise<CheckVersion[]> {
184	  const { data } = await api.get<CheckVersion[]>(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks/${checkId}/versions`);
185	  return data;
186	}
187	
188	/**
189	 * Mirrors the backend `CheckResultPointRead` — one past result of a check
190	 * (status + `metric_value` + run time), the datum behind the per-check trend
191	 * chart (ADR 0022). `metric_value` is null for checks that record no metric.
192	 */
193	export interface CheckResultPoint {
194	  run_id: string;
195	  status: string;
196	  metric_value: number | null;
197	  created_at: string;
198	}
199	
200	/**
201	 * A check's recent results in chronological order (oldest→newest) for the trend
202	 * chart. Requires `view` on the suite; `limit` caps the window (1–180, default 30).
203	 */
204	export async function listCheckHistory(
205	  suiteId: string,
206	  checkId: string,
207	  limit?: number,
208	): Promise<CheckResultPoint[]> {
209	  const { data } = await api.get<CheckResultPoint[]>(
210	    `/suites/${suiteId}/checks/${checkId}/history`,
211	    { params: limit ? { limit } : undefined },
212	  );
213	  return data;
214	}
215	
216	// ───────────────────────── export / import (portable documents) ─────
217	
218	/**
219	 * Mirrors the backend `CheckDocument` — a check's authoring fields only, no DB
220	 * identity. Thresholds may arrive as a number or a string (the backend's
221	 * `Decimal` JSON encoding is not pinned); kept as-is so the document round-trips
222	 * byte-for-faithful on re-import — never coerce or re-format them.
223	 */
224	export interface CheckDocument {
225	  name: string;
226	  kind: string;
227	  expectation_type: string;
228	  config: Record<string, unknown>;
229	  warn_threshold: number | string | null;
230	  fail_threshold: number | string | null;
231	  critical_threshold: number | string | null;
232	}
233	
234	/** Mirrors the backend `SuiteDocument` — a portable, connection-agnostic suite
235	 *  (the export response and the import payload are the same shape). */
236	export interface SuiteDocument {
237	  version: number;
238	  name: string;
239	  description: string | null;
240	  checks: CheckDocument[];
241	}
242	
243	export async function exportSuite(suiteId: string): Promise<SuiteDocument> {
244	  const { data } = await api.get<SuiteDocument>(`/suites/${suiteId}/export`);
245	  return data;
246	}
247	
248	/** Mirrors `SuiteImportRequest` — import a document onto a target connection
249	 *  (the new suite is owned by the importing user, like create). */
250	export interface SuiteImportRequest {
251	  connection_id: string;
252	  document: SuiteDocument;
253	}
254	
255	export async function importSuite(payload: SuiteImportRequest): Promise<Suite> {
256	  const { data } = await api.post<Suite>('/suites/import', payload);
257	  return data;
258	}

266	  fail_threshold?: number | null;
267	  critical_threshold?: number | null;
268	  table: string;
269	  schema?: string | null;
270	}
271	
272	/** Mirrors `CheckDryRunResult` — the preview outcome (severity tier + metric). */
273	export interface CheckDryRunResult {

275	  metric_value: number | null;
276	  observed_value: Record<string, unknown> | null;
277	  expected_value: Record<string, unknown> | null;
278	}
279	
280	export async function dryRunCheck(

282	  payload: CheckDryRunRequest,
283	): Promise<CheckDryRunResult> {

```
import { api } from './client';

/**
 * Connections API — the six configurable connection types (CLAUDE.md §4).
 * ADF + Airflow are orchestration providers, not datasources, but they are
 * still `connections` rows and managed through the same CRUD surface.
 */

export const CONNECTION_TYPES = [
  'snowflake',
  'adls_gen2',
  's3',
  'unity_catalog',
  'adf',
  'airflow',
] as const;
export type ConnectionType = (typeof CONNECTION_TYPES)[number];

/**
 * Datasource vs orchestration is the load-bearing distinction in DataQ
 * (CLAUDE.md §4): datasources are stores you write checks against; ADF/Airflow
 * are orchestration providers we monitor + trigger from, never queryable. This
 * map is the single source for that split — the add-connection picker and the
 * sectioned list both derive their groups from it (no hardcoded lists elsewhere).
 */
export const CONNECTION_KINDS = ['datasource', 'orchestration'] as const;
export type ConnectionKind = (typeof CONNECTION_KINDS)[number];

export const CONNECTION_KIND: Record<ConnectionType, ConnectionKind> = {
  snowflake: 'datasource',
  adls_gen2: 'datasource',
  s3: 'datasource',
  unity_catalog: 'datasource',
  adf: 'orchestration',
  airflow: 'orchestration',
};

export const CONNECTION_KIND_LABELS: Record<ConnectionKind, string> = {
  datasource: 'Data sources',
  orchestration: 'Orchestration',
};

/** Types of a given kind, in canonical CONNECTION_TYPES order. */
export const typesOfKind = (kind: ConnectionKind): ConnectionType[] =>
  CONNECTION_TYPES.filter((t) => CONNECTION_KIND[t] === kind);

export const DATASOURCE_TYPES = typesOfKind('datasource');
export const ORCHESTRATION_TYPES = typesOfKind('orchestration');

/**
 * Datasources GX can run a custom-SQL (`UnexpectedRowsExpectation`) query against
 * — mirrors the backend `custom_sql.SQL_QUERYABLE_TYPES` (ADR 0019). The custom-SQL
 * check category is offered only for these SQL-queryable types; flat files (ADLS /
 * S3) are DataFrame assets, not SQL, and the backend 422s custom-SQL on any other.
 */
export const SQL_QUERYABLE_TYPES: ConnectionType[] = ['snowflake', 'unity_catalog'];

export const isSqlQueryable = (type: ConnectionType): boolean => SQL_QUERYABLE_TYPES.includes(type);

export const CONNECTION_ENVS = ['dev', 'qa', 'uat', 'prod'] as const;
export type ConnectionEnv = (typeof CONNECTION_ENVS)[number];

/** Display label for an env (single source for the list page + the drawer). */
export const envLabel = (env: ConnectionEnv): string => env.toUpperCase();

/** Tag color per env — shared by every page that renders an env badge. */
export const ENV_COLORS: Record<ConnectionEnv, string> = {
  dev: 'blue',
  qa: 'gold',
  uat: 'purple',
  prod: 'red',
};

/** Mirrors the backend `ConnectionRead` schema (secret is never returned). */
export interface Connection {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  type: ConnectionType;
  env: ConnectionEnv;
  config: Record<string, unknown>;
```

/** Human-readable labels for the connection types, for grouping + display. */
export const CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS: Record<ConnectionType, string> = {
  snowflake: 'Snowflake',
  adls_gen2: 'ADLS Gen2',
  s3: 'AWS S3',
  unity_catalog: 'Unity Catalog',
  adf: 'Azure Data Factory',
  airflow: 'Airflow',
};

export async function listConnections(params?: {
  type?: ConnectionType;
  env?: ConnectionEnv;
}): Promise<Connection[]> {
  const { data } = await api.get<Connection[]>('/connections', { params });
  return data;
}

/** Fetch one connection by id (e.g. to learn a suite's datasource type). */
export async function getConnection(id: string): Promise<Connection> {
  const { data } = await api.get<Connection>(`/connections/${id}`);
  return data;
}

/** Live connectivity test — a green result means the credential authenticates. */
export async function testConnection(id: string): Promise<{ ok: boolean }> {
  const { data } = await api.post<{ ok: boolean }>(`/connections/${id}/test`);
  return data;
}

/** Mirrors the backend `ConnectionCreate` schema (secret is write-only). */
export interface ConnectionCreate {
  name: string;
  type: ConnectionType;
  env: ConnectionEnv;
  config: Record<string, unknown>;
  secret?: string;
}

export async function createConnection(payload: ConnectionCreate): Promise<Connection> {
  const { data } = await api.post<Connection>('/connections', payload);
  return data;
}

/** Mirrors the backend `ConnectionUpdate` schema — type/env are immutable. */
export interface ConnectionUpdate {
  name?: string;
  config?: Record<string, unknown>;
  secret?: string;
}

export async function updateConnection(id: string, payload: ConnectionUpdate): Promise<Connection> {
  const { data } = await api.patch<Connection>(`/connections/${id}`, payload);
  return data;
}
```

1	import type { ResultStatus, RunStatus } from '../../api/runs';
2	
3	/**
4	 * Pure presentation helpers for the Results surface — kept framework-free so the
5	 * status→colour mapping and the timing formatters can be unit-tested without
6	 * rendering antd.
7	 */
8	
9	/** antd Tag colour per run execution status. */
10	export const RUN_STATUS_COLORS: Record<RunStatus, string> = {
11	  queued: 'default',
12	  running: 'processing',
13	  succeeded: 'success',
14	  failed: 'error',
15	  cancelled: 'warning',
16	};
17	
18	/**
19	 * antd `Progress` bar status per run lifecycle state. A `Record` (not a switch)
20	 * so a new `RunStatus` value is a compile error here rather than silently
21	 * falling through to `'normal'`.
22	 */
23	export const RUN_BAR_STATUS: Record<RunStatus, 'success' | 'exception' | 'active' | 'normal'> = {
24	  queued: 'normal',
25	  running: 'active',
26	  succeeded: 'success',
27	  failed: 'exception',
28	  cancelled: 'exception',
29	};
30	
31	/** antd Tag colour per result severity / operational status (ADR 0005 / #122). */
32	export const RESULT_STATUS_COLORS: Record<ResultStatus, string> = {
33	  pass: 'success',
34	  warn: 'warning',
35	  fail: 'error',
36	  critical: 'magenta',
37	  skip: 'default',
38	  error: 'volcano',
39	};
40	
41	/** Orchestrator pipeline-run status → colour (provider-agnostic value set). */
42	export function pipelineStatusColor(status: string): string {
43	  switch (status) {
44	    case 'succeeded':
45	      return 'success';
46	    case 'failed':
47	      return 'error';
48	    case 'running':
49	      return 'processing';
50	    case 'cancelled':
51	      return 'warning';
52	    default:
53	      return 'default';
54	  }
55	}
56	
57	/**
58	 * Render an unknown scalar (a GX observed/expected value, a profiled min/max or
59	 * top value) for display: an em dash for null/undefined, JSON for objects, the
60	 * `String` form otherwise. Falsy scalars (`0`, `false`, `''`) render as
61	 * themselves — not collapsed to the em dash — so a real zero isn't mistaken for
62	 * "no value". The em-dash sentinel matches `formatTimestamp` / `formatDuration`.
63	 */
64	export function formatScalar(value: unknown): string {
65	  if (value === null || value === undefined) return '—';
66	  return typeof value === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(value) : String(value);
67	}
68	
69	/** Absolute timestamp as a locale string, or an em dash when absent. */
70	export function formatTimestamp(iso: string | null): string {
71	  if (!iso) return '—';
72	  const d = new Date(iso);
73	  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? '—' : d.toLocaleString();
74	}
75	
76	/**
77	 * Run duration (finished − started) as a compact human string: `850ms`, `12s`,
78	 * `1m 3s`. Returns an em dash when either bound is missing (queued / never
79	 * finished) or the interval is negative (clock skew).
80	 */
81	export function formatDuration(startedAt: string | null, finishedAt: string | null): string {
82	  if (!startedAt || !finishedAt) return '—';
83	  const ms = new Date(finishedAt).getTime() - new Date(startedAt).getTime();
84	  if (Number.isNaN(ms) || ms < 0) return '—';
85	  if (ms < 1000) return `${ms}ms`;
86	  const totalSeconds = Math.round(ms / 1000);
87	  if (totalSeconds < 60) return `${totalSeconds}s`;
88	  const minutes = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 60);
89	  const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;
90	  return `${minutes}m ${seconds}s`;
91	}
92

'var(--bg-hover)' : 'transparent',\n          fontFamily: 'var(--font-sans)', fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text-body)',\n        }}>\n        <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-muted)', display: 'inline-flex' }}>{icon}</span>{label}\n      </button>\n    );\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div ref={ref} style={{ position: 'relative' }}>\n      <Button variant=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\"\n        iconLeft={<window.Icon.download size={14} />}\n        iconRight={<window.Icon.down size={11} style={{ marginLeft: -2 }} />}\n        onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}>Download</Button>\n      {open && (\n        <div style={{\n          position: 'absolute', right: 0, top: 'calc(100% + 6px)', width: 196, zIndex: 30,\n          background: 'var(--bg-card)', border: '1px solid var(--border)', borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)',\n          boxShadow: 'var(--shadow-overlay)', padding: 6,\n        }}>\n          <Item icon={<window.Icon.docs size={15} />} label=\"Download as CSV\" onClick={downloadCsv} />\n          <Item icon={<window.Icon.docs size={15} />} label=\"Download as JSON\" onClick={downloadJson} />\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction RunDetailScreen({ go, params }) {\n  const { Card, MetricCard, StatusBadge, DataTable, Button } = DQ;\n  const { PageHeader } = window;\n  const run = (params && params.run) || RUNS[0];\n  const started = run.started || '—';\n  const startParts = started.split(', ');\n  const cols = [\n    { key: 'check', header: 'Check', render: (v) => <strong style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)', fontWeight: 600 }}>{v}</strong> },\n    { key: 'exp', header: 'Expectation', mono: true, render: (v) => <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-secondary)', fontSize: 12 }}>{v}</span> },\n    { key: 'status', header: 'Status', width: 96, render: (v) => <StatusBadge status={v} variant=\"soft\" /> },\n    { key: 'metric', header: 'Metric', align: 'right', width: 70, mono: true },\n    { key: 'observed', header: 'Observed', mono: true, render: (v) => <span style={{ fontSize: 12, color: 'var(--text-body)' }}>{v}</span> },\n  ];\n  const passed = CHECK_RESULTS.filter((c) => c.status === 'pass').length;\n  return (\n    <div style={{ maxWidth: 1000, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <PageHeader backLabel=\"Results\" onBack={() => go('results')}\n        title={run.suite} subtitle={<span style={{ fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono)' }}>{run.by || 'manual'}</span>}\n        actions={<React.Fragment>\n          <DownloadMenu run={run} />\n          <Button variant=\"secondary\" size=\"sm\" iconLeft={<window.Icon.refresh size={14} />}>Re-run</Button>\n        </React.Fragment>} />\n\n      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 16, marginBottom: 20 }}>\n        <Card padding={18}>\n          <div style={{ fontSize: 13, color: 'var(--text-secondary)', marginBottom: 8 }}>Status</div>\n          <StatusBadge status={run.status} variant=\"soft\" />\n        </Card>\n        <MetricCard label=\"Checks passed\" value={`${passed}/${CHECK_RESULTS.length}`} progress={(passed / CHECK_RESULTS.length) * 100} progressTone={passed === CHECK_RESULTS.length ? 'pass' : 'warning'} />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Started\" value={startParts[1] || startParts[0]} footnote={startParts[1] ? startParts[0] : null} />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Duration\" value={run.dur || '—'} />\n      </div>\n\n      <div style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)', marginBottom: 12 }}>Check results</div>\n      <Card padding={0} style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}><DataTable columns={cols} rows={CHECK_RESULTS} /></Card>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction PipelinesTab() {\n  const { DataTable, StatusBadge, Tag } = DQ;\n  const cols = [\n    { key: 'provider', header: 'Provider', width: 110, render: (v) => <Tag tone=\"outline\">{v}</Tag> },\n    { key: 'dag', header: 'Pipeline / DAG', mono: true, render: (v) => <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-primary)' }}>{v}</span> },\n    { key: 'env', header: 'Env', width: 80, render: (v) => <Tag>{v}</Tag> },\n    { key: 'status', header: 'Status', width: 130, render: (v) => <StatusBadge status={v} variant=\"soft\" /> },\n    { key: 'started', header: 'Started', nowrap: true },\n    { key: 'reason', header: 'Failure reason', render: (v) => <span style={{ color: v === '—' ? 'var(--text-muted)' : 'var(--status-fail)' }}>{v}</span> },\n  ];\n  return <DQ.Card padding={0} style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}><DataTable columns={cols} rows={PIPELINES} /></DQ.Card>;\n}\n\nfunction ResultsScreen({ go }) {\n  const { MetricCard } = DQ;\n  const I = window.Icon;\n  const [tab, setTab] = React.useState('runs');\n  return (\n    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 20, maxWidth: 1200, margin: '0 auto' }}>\n      <h1 style={{ margin: 0, fontFamily: 'var(--font-sans)', fontSize: 24, fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-primary)', letterSpacing: '-0.005em' }}>Results</h1>\n      <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 16 }}>\n        <MetricCard label=\"Pass Rate (7d)\" value=\"94.2%\" delta=\"+1.2%\" trend=\"up\" icon={<I.shield size={18} />} progress={94.2} />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Runs (7d)\" value=\"1,204\" icon={<I.refresh size={18} />} footnote=\"Across 42 suites\" />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Open Anomalies\" value=\"128\" delta=\"-14%\" trend=\"down\" icon={<I.alert size={18} />} footnote=\"warn + fail + critical\" />\n        <MetricCard label=\"Avg. Duration\" value=\"2.4h\" delta=\"-20m\" trend=\"down\" icon={<I.clock size={18} />} />\n      </div>\n      <div>\n        <Tabs value={tab} onChange={setTab} items={[{ key: 'runs', label: 'Runs' }, { key: 'pipelines', label: 'Pipeline runs' }]} />\n        <div style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>{tab === 'runs' ? <RunsTab onOpen={(run) => go('run-detail', { run })} /> : <PipelinesTab />}</div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nObject.assign(window, { ResultsScreen, RunDetailScreen });\n","contentType":"application/javascript","isBase64":false,"truncated":false,"notice":"Upgraded your claude.ai login to include design-system access (user:design:read, user:design:write). This lets /design-sync read and write your org's design-system projects on claude.ai/design."}

> AGENT

The prototype's ResultsScreen has no filter bar — so Phase 3.1 is our own enrichment (status + provider already shipped beyond the prototype). I have design latitude here.

Key constraint I found: **dayjs is not installed** and antd v6 doesn't bundle it, so a true `RangePicker` would mean adding a new dependency (+ bundle/audit cost). The dashboard already uses a preset window control (24h/7d/30d). I'll match that pattern for the date filter — a preset window Select — keeping it dependency-free and consistent. I'll flag this so you can ask for a true range picker if you'd rather.

Let me set up the branch and todos, then implement 3.1.

id: toolu_01LMJHiwZiVSSvveT9GNtsey
```
1	import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
2	
3	/** Three-state result of an async fetch. */
4	export type AsyncState<T> =
5	  | { status: 'loading' }
6	  | { status: 'ok'; data: T }
7	  | { status: 'error'; error: string };
8	
9	/**
10	 * Fetch on mount (and on `reload()`), with a cancelled-guard so a late
11	 * resolution after unmount doesn't set state, and rejection normalised to a
12	 * string `error`. Shared by the data pages (Home, Connections, …) so the
13	 * cancelled-effect dance lives in one place rather than being re-derived per page.
14	 *
15	 * `reload` re-runs the fetcher (e.g. after a mutation) while keeping the current
16	 * data visible until the refetch resolves — no flash back to the loading state.
17	 */
18	export function useAsyncData<T>(fetcher: () => Promise<T>): {
19	  state: AsyncState<T>;
20	  reload: () => void;
21	} {
22	  const [state, setState] = useState<AsyncState<T>>({ status: 'loading' });
23	  const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0);
24	
25	  useEffect(() => {
26	    let cancelled = false;
27	    fetcher()
28	      .then((data) => {
29	        if (!cancelled) setState({ status: 'ok', data });
30	      })
31	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
32	        if (!cancelled) {
33	          setState({ status: 'error', error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
34	        }
35	      });
36	    return () => {
37	      cancelled = true;
38	    };
39	    // Re-run on mount and whenever `reload` bumps the nonce; the fetcher identity
40	    // is intentionally not a dependency.
41	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
42	  }, [nonce]);
43	
44	  const reload = useCallback(() => setNonce((n) => n + 1), []);
45	  return { state, reload };
46	}
47	
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/components/results/resultsFormat.ts",
  "old_string": "/** Absolute timestamp as a locale string, or an em dash when absent. */",
  "new_string": "/**\n * True when `iso` falls within the last `windowDays` days (inclusive). Used by\n * the Results date filter; a null/unparseable timestamp is treated as out of\n * window so rows with no date never leak past a date filter. Kept pure so it can\n * be unit-tested without rendering.\n */\nexport function isWithinWindowDays(iso: string | null, windowDays: number): boolean {\n  if (!iso) return false;\n  const t = new Date(iso).getTime();\n  if (Number.isNaN(t)) return false;\n  return t >= Date.now() - windowDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n}\n\n/** Absolute timestamp as a locale string, or an em dash when absent. */"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/pages/Results.tsx",
  "old_string": "function RunsTab() {\n  // Fetch a page of runs + the accessible suites (for id→name), then filter by\n  // status client-side — cheap at this volume and avoids a refetch per filter.\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => listRuns({ limit: LIST_LIMIT }));\n  const { state: suitesState } = useAsyncData(listSuites);\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState<RunStatus | 'all'>('all');\n\n  const suiteNames = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, string>();\n    if (suitesState.status === 'ok') {\n      for (const s of suitesState.data) map.set(s.id, s.name);\n    }\n    return map;\n  }, [suitesState]);\n\n  if (state.status === 'loading') return <Spin tip=\"Loading runs…\" size=\"large\" />;\n  if (state.status === 'error') {\n    return <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon message=\"Failed to load runs\" description={state.error} />;\n  }\n\n  const runs = status === 'all' ? state.data : state.data.filter((r) => r.status === status);\n\n  const columns: ColumnsType<Run> = [\n    {\n      title: 'Suite',\n      dataIndex: 'suite_id',\n      render: (suiteId: string) =>\n        suiteNames.get(suiteId) ?? <Typography.Text code>{suiteId.slice(0, 8)}</Typography.Text>,\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Status',\n      dataIndex: 'status',\n      width: 120,\n      render: (s: RunStatus) => <Tag color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[s]}>{s}</Tag>,\n    },\n    { title: 'Triggered by', dataIndex: 'triggered_by', render: (t: string | null) => t ?? '—' },\n    {\n      title: 'Started',\n      dataIndex: 'started_at',\n      render: (t: string | null) => formatTimestamp(t),\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Duration',\n      width: 110,\n      render: (_: unknown, run: Run) => formatDuration(run.started_at, run.finished_at),\n    },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={16}>\n      <Flex gap={12} align=\"center\">\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\">Status</Typography.Text>\n        <Select<RunStatus | 'all'>\n          value={status}\n          onChange={setStatus}\n          style={{ width: 160 }}\n          options={[\n            { value: 'all', label: 'All' },\n            ...RUN_STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: s })),\n          ]}\n        />\n      </Flex>\n      <Table<Run>\n        rowKey=\"id\"\n        columns={columns}\n        dataSource={runs}\n        pagination={false}\n        locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description=\"No runs yet.\" /> }}\n        onRow={(run) => ({\n          onClick: () => navigate(`/results/${run.id}`),\n          style: { cursor: 'pointer' },\n        })}\n      />\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Per-suite facts the run filters need: display name + the env / datasource\n *  category of the suite's connection (a run only carries `suite_id`). */\ninterface SuiteMeta {\n  name: string;\n  env: ConnectionEnv | null;\n  category: DatasourceCategory | null;\n}\n\nfunction RunsTab() {\n  // Fetch a page of runs + the accessible suites + connections (for id→name and\n  // the env / datasource of each suite), then filter client-side — cheap at this\n  // volume and avoids a refetch per filter change.\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => listRuns({ limit: LIST_LIMIT }));\n  const { state: suitesState } = useAsyncData(listSuites);\n  const { state: connectionsState } = useAsyncData(() => listConnections());\n\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState<RunStatus | 'all'>('all');\n  const [suiteId, setSuiteId] = useState<string | 'all'>('all');\n  const [env, setEnv] = useState<ConnectionEnv | 'all'>('all');\n  const [category, setCategory] = useState<DatasourceCategory | 'all'>('all');\n  const [dateWindow, setDateWindow] = useState<DateWindow>('all');\n\n  // suite_id → { name, env, datasource category }, joining suites to their\n  // connection. Missing connection (still loading / inaccessible) → null facts.\n  const suiteMeta = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, SuiteMeta>();\n    if (suitesState.status !== 'ok') return map;\n    const conns = connectionsState.status === 'ok' ? connectionsState.data : [];\n    const connById = new Map(conns.map((c) => [c.id, c]));\n    for (const s of suitesState.data) {\n      const conn = connById.get(s.connection_id);\n      map.set(s.id, {\n        name: s.name,\n        env: conn?.env ?? null,\n        category: conn ? DATASOURCE_CATEGORY[conn.type] : null,\n      });\n    }\n    return map;\n  }, [suitesState, connectionsState]);\n\n  // Suite options sorted by name — the filter offers every accessible suite, not\n  // only those with runs in the current page.\n  const suiteOptions = useMemo(\n    () =>\n      [...suiteMeta.entries()]\n        .map(([id, meta]) => ({ value: id, label: meta.name }))\n        .sort((a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label)),\n    [suiteMeta],\n  );\n\n  if (state.status === 'loading') return <Spin tip=\"Loading runs…\" size=\"large\" />;\n  if (state.status === 'error') {\n    return <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon message=\"Failed to load runs\" description={state.error} />;\n  }\n\n  const windowDays = dateWindow === 'all' ? null : Number(dateWindow);\n  const runs = state.data.filter((r) => {\n    if (status !== 'all' && r.status !== status) return false;\n    if (suiteId !== 'all' && r.suite_id !== suiteId) return false;\n    const meta = suiteMeta.get(r.suite_id);\n    if (env !== 'all' && meta?.env !== env) return false;\n    if (category !== 'all' && meta?.category !== category) return false;\n    if (windowDays !== null && !isWithinWindowDays(r.started_at ?? r.created_at, windowDays))\n      return false;\n    return true;\n  });\n\n  const columns: ColumnsType<Run> = [\n    {\n      title: 'Suite',\n      dataIndex: 'suite_id',\n      render: (id: string) =>\n        suiteMeta.get(id)?.name ?? <Typography.Text code>{id.slice(0, 8)}</Typography.Text>,\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Status',\n      dataIndex: 'status',\n      width: 120,\n      render: (s: RunStatus) => <Tag color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[s]}>{s}</Tag>,\n    },\n    { title: 'Triggered by', dataIndex: 'triggered_by', render: (t: string | null) => t ?? '—' },\n    {\n      title: 'Started',\n      dataIndex: 'started_at',\n      render: (t: string | null) => formatTimestamp(t),\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Duration',\n      width: 110,\n      render: (_: unknown, run: Run) => formatDuration(run.started_at, run.finished_at),\n    },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={16}>\n      <Flex gap={16} align=\"flex-end\" wrap=\"wrap\">\n        <Filter label=\"Status\">\n          <Select<RunStatus | 'all'>\n            value={status}\n            onChange={setStatus}\n            style={{ width: 150 }}\n            options={[\n              { value: 'all', label: 'All' },\n              ...RUN_STATUSES.map((s) => ({ value: s, label: s })),\n            ]}\n          />\n        </Filter>\n        <Filter label=\"Suite\">\n          <Select<string | 'all'>\n            value={suiteId}\n            onChange={setSuiteId}\n            style={{ width: 220 }}\n            showSearch\n            optionFilterProp=\"label\"\n            options={[{ value: 'all', label: 'All suites' }, ...suiteOptions]}\n          />\n        </Filter>\n        <Filter label=\"Environment\">\n          <Select<ConnectionEnv | 'all'>\n            value={env}\n            onChange={setEnv}\n            style={{ width: 130 }}\n            options={[\n              { value: 'all', label: 'All' },\n              ...CONNECTION_ENVS.map((e) => ({ value: e, label: envLabel(e) })),\n            ]}\n          />\n        </Filter>\n        <Filter label=\"Datasource\">\n          <Select<DatasourceCategory | 'all'>\n            value={category}\n            onChange={setCategory}\n            style={{ width: 160 }}\n            options={[\n              { value: 'all', label: 'All' },\n              ...DATASOURCE_CATEGORIES.map((c) => ({\n                value: c,\n                label: DATASOURCE_CATEGORY_LABELS[c],\n              })),\n            ]}\n          />\n        </Filter>\n        <Filter label=\"Date\">\n          <Select<DateWindow>\n            value={dateWindow}\n            onChange={setDateWindow}\n            style={{ width: 150 }}\n            options={DATE_WINDOWS.map((w) => ({ value: w.value, label: w.label }))}\n          />\n        </Filter>\n      </Flex>\n      <Table<Run>\n        rowKey=\"id\"\n        columns={columns}\n        dataSource={runs}\n        pagination={false}\n        locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description=\"No runs match these filters.\" /> }}\n        onRow={(run) => ({\n          onClick: () => navigate(`/results/${run.id}`),\n          style: { cursor: 'pointer' },\n        })}\n      />\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}"
}

1	import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
2	
3	import {
4	  formatDuration,

6	  formatTimestamp,

13	describe('formatScalar', () => {
14	  it('returns an em dash for null or undefined', () => {
15	    expect(formatScalar(null)).toBe('—');
16	    expect(formatScalar(undefined)).toBe('—');
17	  });
18	
19	  it('renders falsy scalars as themselves, not the em dash', () => {
20	    expect(formatScalar(0)).toBe('0');
21	    expect(formatScalar(false)).toBe('false');
22	    expect(formatScalar('')).toBe('');
23	  });
24	
25	  it('JSON-stringifies objects and arrays', () => {
26	    expect(formatScalar({ a: 1 })).toBe('{"a":1}');
27	    expect(formatScalar([1, 2])).toBe('[1,2]');
28	  });
29	
30	  it('stringifies plain scalars', () => {
31	    expect(formatScalar('PUBLIC')).toBe('PUBLIC');
32	    expect(formatScalar(9999)).toBe('9999');
33	  });
34	});
35	
36	describe('formatDuration', () => {
37	  it('returns an em dash when either bound is missing', () => {
38	    expect(formatDuration(null, '2026-06-11T00:00:10Z')).toBe('—');
39	    expect(formatDuration('2026-06-11T00:00:00Z', null)).toBe('—');
40	    expect(formatDuration(null, null)).toBe('—');
41	  });
42	
43	  it('returns an em dash for a negative interval (clock skew)', () => {
44	    expect(formatDuration('2026-06-11T00:00:10Z', '2026-06-11T00:00:00Z')).toBe('—');
45	  });
46	
47	  it('formats sub-second, seconds, and minute+second spans', () => {
48	    expect(formatDuration('2026-06-11T00:00:00.000Z', '2026-06-11T00:00:00.850Z')).toBe('850ms');
49	    expect(formatDuration('2026-06-11T00:00:00Z', '2026-06-11T00:00:12Z')).toBe('12s');
50	    expect(formatDuration('2026-06-11T00:00:00Z', '2026-06-11T00:01:03Z')).toBe('1m 3s');
51	  });
52	});
53	
54	describe('formatTimestamp', () => {
55	  it('returns an em dash for null or unparseable input', () => {
56	    expect(formatTimestamp(null)).toBe('—');

58	  });
59	
60	  it('returns a non-empty locale string for a valid ISO timestamp', () => {

> AGENT

Phase 3.1 (filter bar expansion) is done and committed. Here's the summary:

## Phase 3.1 — Results filter bar expansion ✅ `4f7c81b`

**What shipped** — the `/results` runs tab now has five filters (was just status from PR-C1):

| Filter | Source | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| Status | `run.status` | existing |
| Suite | accessible suites | searchable; lists all suites, not only those with runs |
| Environment | suite → connection `env` | dev/qa/uat/prod |
| Datasource | suite → connection `type` | ADLS+S3 collapsed into one **Flat file** category |
| Date | `started_at ?? created_at` | preset windows 24h/7d/30d |

**Key decisions:**
- A run only carries `suite_id`, so env/datasource are joined via the suite's connection ([Results.tsx](frontend/src/pages/Results.tsx) now fetches `listConnections` too and builds a `suite_id → {name, env, category}` map).
- New reusable `DATASOURCE_CATEGORY` map in [connections.ts](frontend/src/api/connections.ts) — single source for the snowflake / flatfile / unity_catalog grouping.
- **Date = preset windows, not a true `RangePicker`** — antd v6 doesn't bundle dayjs and it's not installed, so a range picker would mean a new dependency (+ bundle/audit cost). The preset window matches the dashboard's existing control. If you'd rather have a true from/to range picker, I can add dayjs + `RangePicker` instead — just say so.

**Verification:** lint + typecheck clean; full suite **256/256 green** (4 new filter tests + pure `isWithinWindowDays` unit tests). Only the 5 intended files changed.

Two things before I move on:

1. **3.2 (orchestration panel polish)** is the other half of Phase 3 — 30s auto-poll on the Pipeline-runs tab + correlate pipeline run → triggered DQ run via `triggered_by`. Want me to continue into it now (separate commit, per one-functionality-per-commit)?
2. Per your working agreements, `/code-review` agents run before merge. I haven't opened a PR yet — I can keep stacking Phase 3 commits on this branch and open the PR + run review once 3.2 lands, or open it now for 3.1 alone. Which do you prefer?

> DEVELOPER

continue with 3.2

1	import {
2	  App,
3	  Alert,
4	  Button,
5	  Drawer,
6	  Empty,
7	  Flex,
8	  List,
9	  Progress,
10	  Spin,
11	  Tag,
12	  Typography,
13	} from 'antd';
14	import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
15	import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
16	
17	import {
18	  cancelRun,
19	  getRunProgress,
20	  type ResultStatus,
21	  type RunProgress,
22	  type RunStatus,
23	} from '../../api/runs';
24	import { RESULT_STATUS_COLORS, RUN_BAR_STATUS, RUN_STATUS_COLORS } from '../results/resultsFormat';
25	
26	/** Run lifecycle states past which polling stops. */
27	const TERMINAL: readonly RunStatus[] = ['succeeded', 'failed', 'cancelled'];
28	const DEFAULT_POLL_MS = 1500;
29	
30	function isTerminal(status: RunStatus): boolean {
31	  return TERMINAL.includes(status);
32	}
33	
34	/**
35	 * Live run-progress drawer — opens on a queued run and polls
36	 * `GET /runs/{id}/progress` until the run is terminal, showing the run
37	 * lifecycle, a completed/total bar, and per-check status (a spinner while a
38	 * check is still pending). An editor can cancel an in-flight run.
39	 *
40	 * Mounted controlled by `runId`; pass `null` to keep it closed. The body is
41	 * keyed by `runId` so opening a different run remounts and restarts polling
42	 * rather than continuing the prior run's loop.
43	 */
44	export function LiveRunProgress({
45	  runId,
46	  suiteName,
47	  canManage,
48	  pollMs = DEFAULT_POLL_MS,
49	  onClose,
50	}: {
51	  runId: string | null;
52	  suiteName: string | null;
53	  canManage: boolean;
54	  /** Poll interval; overridable so tests can drive it deterministically. */
55	  pollMs?: number;
56	  onClose: () => void;
57	}) {
58	  return (
59	    <Drawer
60	      open={runId !== null}
61	      onClose={onClose}
62	      width={560}
63	      title={`Run progress${suiteName ? ` · ${suiteName}` : ''}`}
64	      destroyOnClose
65	    >
66	      {runId !== null && (
67	        <LiveRunProgressBody key={runId} runId={runId} canManage={canManage} pollMs={pollMs} />
68	      )}
69	    </Drawer>
70	  );
71	}
72	
73	function LiveRunProgressBody({
74	  runId,
75	  canManage,
76	  pollMs,
77	}: {
78	  runId: string;
79	  canManage: boolean;
80	  pollMs: number;
81	}) {
82	  const { message } = App.useApp();
83	  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<RunProgress | null>(null);
84	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
85	  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(false);
86	  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
87	  // `stopped` latches once the run reaches a terminal state (poll-observed or
88	  // cancel-forced). It guards against a poll that was already in flight before a
89	  // cancel resolving afterwards and clobbering the terminal status back to
90	  // `running` (cancel is cooperative — the next poll can briefly still read the
91	  // pre-cancel state). Terminal is sticky.
92	  const stoppedRef = useRef(false);
93	
94	  // Poll until terminal via a self-scheduling timeout (not setInterval, so a slow
95	  // request can't pile up overlapping fetches). `active` guards a late resolution
96	  // after unmount. A transient fetch error keeps polling (the live view
97	  // self-heals when the endpoint recovers) — only a terminal status stops it.
98	  useEffect(() => {
99	    let active = true;
100	    stoppedRef.current = false;
101	    const tick = async () => {
102	      try {
103	        const next = await getRunProgress(runId);
104	        if (!active || stoppedRef.current) return;
105	        setProgress(next);
106	        setError(null);
107	        if (isTerminal(next.status)) {
108	          stoppedRef.current = true;
109	        } else {
110	          timerRef.current = setTimeout(tick, pollMs);
111	        }
112	      } catch (err) {
113	        if (!active || stoppedRef.current) return;
114	        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
115	        // Keep polling through a transient error rather than freezing the live
116	        // view; the cadence is bounded by pollMs, and a terminal status / unmount
117	        // still stops it.
118	        timerRef.current = setTimeout(tick, pollMs);
119	      }
120	    };
121	    void tick();
122	    return () => {
123	      active = false;
124	      if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
125	    };
126	  }, [runId, pollMs]);
127	
128	  const onCancel = async () => {
129	    setCancelling(true);
130	    try {
131	      const run = await cancelRun(runId);
132	      // Stop polling and latch terminal so an in-flight pre-cancel poll can't
133	      // flip the status back to `running`.
134	      stoppedRef.current = true;
135	      if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
136	      setProgress((p) => (p ? { ...p, status: run.status, finished_at: run.finished_at } : p));
137	      message.success('Run cancelled');
138	    } catch (err) {
139	      message.error(`Cancel failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
140	    } finally {
141	      setCancelling(false);
142	    }
143	  };
144	
145	  if (!progress) {
146	    if (error) {
147	      return (
148	        <Alert type="error" showIcon message="Failed to load run progress" description={error} />
149	      );
150	    }
151	    return <Spin tip="Starting run…" size="large" />;
152	  }
153	
154	  const { status, total_checks, completed_checks, checks } = progress;
155	  const terminal = isTerminal(status);
156	  const percent = total_checks > 0 ? Math.round((completed_checks / total_checks) * 100) : 0;
157	
158	  return (
159	    <Flex vertical gap={16}>
160	      <Flex gap={12} align="center" wrap>
161	        <Tag color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[status]}>{status}</Tag>
162	        <Typography.Text type="secondary">
163	          {completed_checks} / {total_checks} checks
164	        </Typography.Text>
165	        {canManage && !terminal && (
166	          <Button danger size="small" loading={cancelling} onClick={onCancel}>
167	            Cancel
168	          </Button>
169	        )}
170	      </Flex>
171	
172	      <Progress percent={percent} status={RUN_BAR_STATUS[status]} />
173	
174	      {/* A transient poll error while we still have prior progress to show. */}
175	      {error && (
176	        <Alert type="warning" showIcon message="Progress update failed" description={error} />
177	      )}
178	
179	      {checks.length === 0 ? (
180	        <Empty description="This suite has no checks to run." />
181	      ) : (
182	        <List<(typeof checks)[number]>
183	          size="small"
184	          dataSource={checks}
185	          rowKey="check_id"
186	          renderItem={(c) => (
187	            <List.Item>
188	              <Typography.Text>{c.name}</Typography.Text>
189	              <CheckStatus status={c.status} terminal={terminal} />
190	            </List.Item>
191	          )}
192	        />
193	      )}
194	
195	      {/* Always offer the persistent results surface — the drawer can be closed
196	          mid-run, and (unlike the old navigate-on-run) it's the only in-app path
197	          back to this run until the recent-runs table lands. */}
198	      <Link to="/results">View full results →</Link>
199	    </Flex>
200	  );
201	}
202	
203	/**
204	 * A check's status cell: a resolved check shows its severity tag; a pending
205	 * check spins *while the run is live*, but on a terminal run (a check that never
206	 * produced a result — e.g. a cancelled run) it shows a neutral "not run" rather
207	 * than an eternal spinner.
208	 */
209	function CheckStatus({ status, terminal }: { status: ResultStatus | null; terminal: boolean }) {
210	  if (status === null) {
211	    if (terminal) {
212	      return <Typography.Text type="secondary">not run</Typography.Text>;
213	    }
214	    return (
215	      <Flex gap={6} align="center">
216	        <Spin size="small" />
217	        <Typography.Text type="secondary">pending</Typography.Text>
218	      </Flex>
219	    );
220	  }
221	  return <Tag color={RESULT_STATUS_COLORS[status]}>{status}</Tag>;
222	}
223	
```

def _trigger_suites(
    session: Session, *, provider: str, connection: Connection, update: RunUpdate
) -> list[Run]:
    """Create one queued `Run` per enabled `trigger_binding` for a succeeded run.

    Idempotent on the ``triggered_by`` marker ``<provider>:<pipeline>:<run_id>``:
    a replayed event (or a webhook + poll double-delivery) does not spawn a
    second run for the same (suite, pipeline-run).

    Each created run is handed to Celery (``run_suite``) once committed; the
    worker resolves the suite's target (#215) and fails the run cleanly if the
    suite is targetless. A broker failure marks that run ``failed`` rather than
    leaving it stuck ``queued`` (the 10-min poll won't re-dispatch a stale row).
    """
    marker = f"{provider}:{update.pipeline_or_dag_id}:{update.provider_run_id}"
    bindings = list(
        session.scalars(
            select(TriggerBinding).where(
                TriggerBinding.provider == provider,
                TriggerBinding.pipeline_or_dag_id == update.pipeline_or_dag_id,
                TriggerBinding.env == connection.env,
                TriggerBinding.enabled.is_(True),
            )
        )
    )
    created: list[Run] = []
    for binding in bindings:
        already = session.scalar(
            select(Run.id).where(Run.suite_id == binding.suite_id, Run.triggered_by == marker)
        )
        if already is not None:
            continue
        run = Run(suite_id=binding.suite_id, status="queued", triggered_by=marker)
        session.add(run)
        created.append(run)

    if created:
        session.commit()
```

{

  "old_string": "function PipelineRunsTab() {\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => listPipelineRuns({ limit: LIST_LIMIT }));\n  const [provider, setProvider] = useState<'all' | 'adf' | 'airflow'>('all');\n\n  if (state.status === 'loading') return <Spin tip=\"Loading pipeline runs…\" size=\"large\" />;\n  if (state.status === 'error') {\n    return (\n      <Alert\n        type=\"error\"\n        showIcon\n        message=\"Failed to load pipeline runs\"\n        description={state.error}\n      />\n    );\n  }\n\n  const rows = provider === 'all' ? state.data : state.data.filter((p) => p.provider === provider);\n\n  const columns: ColumnsType<PipelineRun> = [\n    { title: 'Provider', dataIndex: 'provider', width: 110, render: (p: string) => <Tag>{p}</Tag> },\n    { title: 'Pipeline / DAG', dataIndex: 'pipeline_or_dag_id' },\n    { title: 'Env', dataIndex: 'env', width: 80, render: (e: string) => e.toUpperCase() },\n    {\n      title: 'Status',\n      dataIndex: 'status',\n      width: 110,\n      render: (s: string) => <Tag color={pipelineStatusColor(s)}>{s}</Tag>,\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Started',\n      dataIndex: 'started_at',\n      render: (t: string | null) => formatTimestamp(t),\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Failure reason',\n      dataIndex: 'failure_reason',\n      render: (r: string | null) => r ?? '—',\n    },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={16}>\n      <Flex gap={12} align=\"center\">\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\">Provider</Typography.Text>\n        <Select<'all' | 'adf' | 'airflow'>\n          value={provider}\n          onChange={setProvider}\n          style={{ width: 160 }}\n          options={[\n            { value: 'all', label: 'All' },\n            { value: 'adf', label: 'ADF' },\n            { value: 'airflow', label: 'Airflow' },\n          ]}\n        />\n      </Flex>\n      <Table<PipelineRun>\n        rowKey=\"id\"\n        columns={columns}\n        dataSource={rows}\n        pagination={false}\n        locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description=\"No pipeline runs monitored yet.\" /> }}\n      />\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Pipeline-runs auto-poll cadence — orchestrator runs move on the minute scale,\n *  so 30s keeps the panel near-live without hammering the API. */\nconst PIPELINE_POLL_MS = 30_000;\n\nfunction PipelineRunsTab({ pollMs = PIPELINE_POLL_MS }: { pollMs?: number }) {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  // Pipeline runs + the DQ runs they triggered, both auto-refreshed so a newly\n  // triggered run shows up against its pipeline run without a manual reload.\n  const { state, reload } = useAsyncData(() => listPipelineRuns({ limit: LIST_LIMIT }));\n  const { state: runsState, reload: reloadRuns } = useAsyncData(() => listRuns({ limit: LIST_LIMIT }));\n  const [provider, setProvider] = useState<'all' | 'adf' | 'airflow'>('all');\n\n  // Refresh both sources on the poll cadence; `reload` keeps the current rows\n  // visible across the refetch (no flash back to the spinner).\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const id = setInterval(() => {\n      reload();\n      reloadRuns();\n    }, pollMs);\n    return () => clearInterval(id);\n  }, [reload, reloadRuns, pollMs]);\n\n  // triggered_by marker → the DQ runs it spawned (one pipeline run can trigger\n  // several, one per binding).\n  const runsByMarker = useMemo(() => {\n    const map = new Map<string, Run[]>();\n    if (runsState.status !== 'ok') return map;\n    for (const r of runsState.data) {\n      if (!r.triggered_by) continue;\n      const list = map.get(r.triggered_by);\n      if (list) list.push(r);\n      else map.set(r.triggered_by, [r]);\n    }\n    return map;\n  }, [runsState]);\n\n  if (state.status === 'loading') return <Spin tip=\"Loading pipeline runs…\" size=\"large\" />;\n  if (state.status === 'error') {\n    return (\n      <Alert\n        type=\"error\"\n        showIcon\n        message=\"Failed to load pipeline runs\"\n        description={state.error}\n      />\n    );\n  }\n\n  const rows = provider === 'all' ? state.data : state.data.filter((p) => p.provider === provider);\n\n  const columns: ColumnsType<PipelineRun> = [\n    { title: 'Provider', dataIndex: 'provider', width: 110, render: (p: string) => <Tag>{p}</Tag> },\n    { title: 'Pipeline / DAG', dataIndex: 'pipeline_or_dag_id' },\n    { title: 'Env', dataIndex: 'env', width: 80, render: (e: string) => e.toUpperCase() },\n    {\n      title: 'Status',\n      dataIndex: 'status',\n      width: 110,\n      render: (s: string) => <Tag color={pipelineStatusColor(s)}>{s}</Tag>,\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'DQ run',\n      width: 160,\n      render: (_: unknown, p: PipelineRun) => {\n        const triggered = runsByMarker.get(pipelineRunMarker(p)) ?? [];\n        if (triggered.length === 0) return <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\">—</Typography.Text>;\n        return (\n          <Flex gap={6} wrap=\"wrap\">\n            {triggered.map((r) => (\n              <Tag\n                key={r.id}\n                color={RUN_STATUS_COLORS[r.status]}\n                style={{ cursor: 'pointer', marginInlineEnd: 0 }}\n                onClick={() => navigate(`/results/${r.id}`)}\n              >\n                {r.status}\n              </Tag>\n            ))}\n          </Flex>\n        );\n      },\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Started',\n      dataIndex: 'started_at',\n      render: (t: string | null) => formatTimestamp(t),\n    },\n    {\n      title: 'Failure reason',\n      dataIndex: 'failure_reason',\n      render: (r: string | null) => r ?? '—',\n    },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={16}>\n      <Flex gap={12} align=\"center\">\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\">Provider</Typography.Text>\n        <Select<'all' | 'adf' | 'airflow'>\n          value={provider}\n          onChange={setProvider}\n          style={{ width: 160 }}\n          options={[\n            { value: 'all', label: 'All' },\n            { value: 'adf', label: 'ADF' },\n            { value: 'airflow', label: 'Airflow' },\n          ]}\n        />\n      </Flex>\n      <Table<PipelineRun>\n        rowKey=\"id\"\n        columns={columns}\n        dataSource={rows}\n        pagination={false}\n        locale={{ emptyText: <Empty description=\"No pipeline runs monitored yet.\" /> }}\n      />\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}"
}

## Phase 3 — Results page enrichment ✅

**3.1 Filter bar expansion** `4f7c81b` — suite / environment / datasource / date filters added to the runs tab (status was already there).

**3.2 Orchestration panel polish** `4996b7a`:
- **30s auto-poll** on the Pipeline-runs tab via `setInterval` + `useAsyncData`'s `reload()` (keeps rows visible across the refetch — no spinner flash). Refreshes both pipeline runs *and* DQ runs so a newly-triggered run appears against its pipeline.

89	> **Auth-boundary discipline (per [ADR 0010](adr/0010-provider-agnostic-infrastructure-seams.md)):** new connection-CRUD endpoints (and every protected route from here on) depend on a generic internal "current user" dependency that returns DataQ's own `User` — they must NOT read MSAL token claims directly in route/service code. Cheap now; expensive to retrofit once dozens of endpoints exist. No new abstraction layer required, just the boundary.

Enforces `(type, env)` uniqueness for orchestrator rows via a **partial unique index** `WHERE type IN ('adf','airflow')` per [#72](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/72) / ADR 0004 — datasources excluded, so Snowflake stays many-per-env. CRUD/API reused unchanged: pure adapter + registry + migration addition.)_
94	- [x] ✅ Connection re-auth endpoint — refresh expired Key Vault token — `POST /connections/{id}/reauth` (`svc.reauth_connection`): rotates the credential through `SecretStore.set` **and** verifies it via the same adapter probe as `/test`, in one step (the gap PATCH+`/test` leave open). Rotation persists before the probe, so a bad new credential surfaces as 502 `connection_test_failed`; a store-write failure is 502 `connection_secret_write_failed` with the old credential untouched. 6 TestClient tests (rotate+verify ok, failed-verify-but-rotation-persists, write-fail 502, 404, secret-required 422). Type-agnostic — applies to all six connection types
95	- [x] ✅ Review `connections.secret_ref` nullability — decide based on Airflow basic-poll / unauthenticated S3 cases ([PR #41 nit](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/41)) — **decision: keep nullable.** It's NULL for the transient flush→secret-write window (create), for credential-less auth (managed-identity/IAM-role, W7, ADR 0010/0011), and for unauthenticated sources. v1 types are all secret-bearing, but presence is enforced in the **service layer** (`test_connection` → 502 without a credential), not the schema — so W7 credential-less modes need no later migration. Recorded as a comment on the `secret_ref` column in `db/models.py`
